{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Design the implementation plan for the CORE of a SIGIL \"phone companion\" — the missing network transport + an installable PWA + device pairing + CLI. Repo: /home/kali/sigil (a local-first, offense-free personal AI orchestrator; doctrine: append-only signed spine, fail-closed WARDEN tiers, local-first, prove-don't-guess, no offensive capability). This is a NEW workstream on a finished engine (Phases 0-8 done). Output a detailed, slice-by-slice plan naming files to create/modify and the existing code to REUSE. Do NOT write code — design only.\n\nGROUND TRUTH from exploration (all verified):\n- `sigil/bridge/daemon.py`: `BridgeDaemon` is a REAL, TESTED library core but has NO network transport. Methods: `pending()->[{seq,tier,kind}]` (minimal, no subject leak), `submit_device_approval(payload)->int` (fail-closed: requires payload signal==governor.approval, pubkey in authorized_devices, verify_approval passes), `panic_engage(by='phone')->int` (kill-switch engage, unsigned SAFE direction; release stays owner-only), `relay(text)->str` (→ voice.dispatch.KernelDispatch().send(text), same WARDEN gate). `bind_ok(addr)->bool` accepts loopback + private/WireGuard, refuses 0.0.0.0/::/public. The module docstring EXPLICITLY says the transport (HTTP over WireGuard, reusing the WS-C two-plane server pattern bound to a bind_ok address) is a 'documented NEXT slice, not shipped'.\n- `sigil/bridge/notifier.py`: `PushNotifier` wraps `SpineTailer`; `poll()->[{seq,tier,kind}]` for newly-queued A2/A3 items only. No cloud, no subject/secret.\n- `sigil/ui/server.py` (WS-C glass cockpit — THE TEMPLATE TO REUSE): a stdlib `http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer` bound to 127.0.0.1. Two planes: GET read-plane (`_token_ok`: hmac.compare_digest of X-SIGIL-Token or ?token=) and POST action-plane (`_action_ok`: Host allowlist {127.0.0.1:port, localhost:port} anti-rebind + exact-match Origin + Referer-under-origin + token). Strict CSP `default-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'`, X-Content-Type-Options nosniff, Referrer-Policy no-referrer, body cap 65536, 30s timeout, token never logged. SSE `/api/stream` via SpineTailer with `?since=` cursor + heartbeat. `/api/snapshot`=dashboard.snapshot(store), `/api/record/{seq}` re-verifies entry_hash LIVE via verify_record, `/api/classify?tool=`, `/api/ask?q=` (carries full action gate). `ui/actions.py` `do_action`: ACTIONS={approve,deny,kill,release,promote,revoke}; the SERVER holds owner key (ensure_owner_keypair) and signs — the browser NEVER holds key material.\n- `sigil/mesh/registry.py`: owner authorizes a phone's Ed25519 device key via `authorize_device(store, device_id, device_pubkey, owner_key)` (owner-signed mesh.device state=authorized); `revoke_device`; `authorized_devices(store, owner_pubkey)->Set[pubkey]` (forged/self-signed/revoked rejected via verify_signed over _DEV_CORE). `DeviceApprover(store, device_key).approve/deny(seq)` signs `_approval_message(seq, decision, approver)` = canonical_json({target,decision,approver}) with the phone's OWN key (never the owner trust-root); appends governor.approval with pubkey/sig/device=True/supersedes_id=seq.\n- `sigil/agents/approvals.py`: `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices)` accepts owner OR authorized-device signatures, target_seq binds (no replay). `pending(store, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=)` is SIGNAL-AGNOSTIC (operator plans, actor steps, gesture actions all appear).\n- No CLI exists for bridge/mesh/pairing. `cli.py` has `serve` (loopback UI), `host` (prints CapabilityDescriptor), `approve`/`deny` (owner-signed). Add commands here.\n- Tests live in `tests/test_*.py`, run as plain scripts (a `__main__` block runs all test_* fns; deterministic, temp SpineStore(tempfile), generate_keypair()). See tests/test_mobile.py and tests/test_ui.py for the exact style to mirror.\n\nDESIGN THESE SLICES (security-first ordering; each is its own reviewable unit):\n1. The WireGuard-bound bridge HTTP transport (new `sigil/bridge/server.py`): reuse the WS-C `ui/server.py` two-plane pattern but bound to a `bind_ok` address (loopback OR WireGuard-private, refuse public/0.0.0.0). Endpoints wrapping BridgeDaemon: GET pending (minimal), GET push-stream (SSE via PushNotifier/SpineTailer), POST submit-device-approval (the phone posts its OWN device-signed governor.approval payload — the server does NOT sign; it verifies via submit_device_approval), POST panic, POST relay. CRITICAL DESIGN QUESTION to resolve: the WS-C action-plane trusts a printed token; here the real authenticator is the DEVICE SIGNATURE on approvals, but panic/relay need their own auth — design how panic/relay are authenticated (device-signed request envelope? a per-device bearer token minted at pairing? both?). Address DNS-rebinding/Origin for a PWA served from the phone over WireGuard (the Origin/Host will be the WireGuard IP, not loopback — how does the allowlist adapt while staying anti-rebind?). Address replay/nonce for panic/relay (approvals are target_seq-bound; panic/relay are not — design a nonce or signed-timestamp envelope).\n2. The device-signed request envelope (new small module, e.g. `sigil/bridge/envelope.py`): a canonical, replay-resistant signed request (device pubkey + monotonic nonce/seq + action + args + sig) verified against authorized_devices — so panic/relay/(future arm) are authenticated the same owner-minted-key way approvals are, without a shared bearer secret. Reuse reuse.sign/verify_one + canonical_json.\n3. The installable PWA (new `sigil/bridge/webapp/`): manifest.json + service-worker.js (offline shell) + index.html + app.js + style.css, ALL self-contained (no CDN — strict CSP, doctrine). It holds the phone's OWN Ed25519 device key in-browser: design key generation (WebCrypto Ed25519 where available, else a VENDORED noble-ed25519 — inline the minified source, no network), storage (IndexedDB; discuss non-extractable vs library-extractable tradeoff), and offline approval SIGNING (reproduce `_approval_message` canonical_json exactly so the browser signature verifies server-side). UI: pending A2/A3 list with tier/kind, approve/deny (sign locally, POST), panic button, NL-relay box, read-only cockpit views (snapshot/record/graph — reuse the read-plane), live push feed (SSE). Provenance-first like WS-C.\n4. Device pairing flow: the phone generates its keypair, displays its PUBLIC key (+ a short human-verifiable fingerprint / QR) — the owner authorizes it at the PC via a new CLI `sigil mesh authorize <device-id> <pubkey>` (calls authorize_device). Design the QR/fingerprint (self-contained JS QR renderer, vendored) and the anti-mistake confirmation. NEVER transmit any private key; the owner trust-root never leaves the PC.\n5. CLI: `sigil bridge serve [--addr <wg-ip>] [--port]` (starts the transport, prints the bind + a pairing hint), `sigil mesh authorize|revoke|list-devices`. Reuse cli.py arg patterns.\n6. Tests (`tests/test_bridge.py`): deterministic, no real sockets where possible (test bind_ok, the envelope verify/replay-refusal, submit-device-approval end-to-end through the daemon, the SSE minimal-payload, pairing authorize/revoke). Mirror tests/test_mobile.py + test_ui.py style. Include NEGATIVE controls: an unauthorized device's approval/panic/relay refused; a replayed envelope refused; a public/0.0.0.0 bind refused; no owner key ever required on the phone side.\n\nReuse-first: name the EXACT functions/classes to reuse (BridgeDaemon, bind_ok, PushNotifier, SpineTailer, verify_approval, authorized_devices, DeviceApprover, ensure_owner_keypair, dashboard.snapshot, verify_record, canonical_json, sign/verify_one, the ui/server.py handler structure). For each slice: files, the security invariants it must preserve, and the adversarial-review lenses (esp. slice 1/2 — the transport auth + replay). Flag any doctrine risk (a bearer secret over the wire, Origin handling for a non-loopback PWA, key storage in the browser). Give a build order and per-slice verification."},"uuid":"066d5de4-46a0-45d4-85cc-bbb9604b46b1","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:07:21.914Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8ef0ffdb-0f1e-4178-a3e5-a87d5e6c64ca","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud (requires claude.ai account access)). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":19,"isInitial":true,"names":["crucible","deep-research","adversarial-sweep","dataviz","artifact-design","artifact-capabilities","update-config","keybindings-help","verify","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","schedule","claude-api","run","init","review","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"d151bf6b-9bd4-4e8f-9f37-5b9f5f26b218","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:07:21.920Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3436ec0e-d821-4fe4-abe7-b0b40e8bd66f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EVLYRXHLjfmDAiPsrnXn8J","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"BridgeDaemon (Phase 7, WS-D D-v) — the desktop-side API the phone talks to over WireGuard. It\n2\treuses the owner-signed cores: the queue (`approvals.pending` with the authorized-device set), a\n3\tDEVICE-signed approval (accepted only while that device is authorized), a PANIC halt (any engage is\n4\tfail-safe), and a KERNEL command relay (`KernelDispatch` → the same WARDEN gate as voice/UI).\n5\t\n6\tThis module is the bridge LIBRARY CORE + API (the doctrine + crypto). The network TRANSPORT — an\n7\tHTTP server over WireGuard, reusing the WS-C two-plane server pattern bound to a `bind_ok` address —\n8\tis a documented NEXT slice, not shipped here. `bind_ok` is the bind predicate that transport MUST\n9\tuse: loopback or a PRIVATE (WireGuard) address only — NEVER 0.0.0.0 / a public address.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport ipaddress\n13\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n16\t\n17\t\n18\tdef bind_ok(addr: str) -> bool:\n19\t    \"\"\"True iff `addr` is safe to bind: loopback or a PRIVATE (e.g. WireGuard) address — never\n20\t    0.0.0.0 / a public / an unspecified address.\"\"\"\n21\t    try:\n22\t        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(addr)\n23\t    except ValueError:\n24\t        return False\n25\t    if ip.is_unspecified:                          # 0.0.0.0 / :: → refuse\n26\t        return False\n27\t    return ip.is_loopback or ip.is_private\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tclass BridgeDaemon:\n31\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n32\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n33\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n34\t        # the owner trust anchor — the persisted identity by default (injectable for tests)\n35\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n36\t\n37\t    def _authorized(self):\n38\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n39\t        return authorized_devices(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey)\n40\t\n41\t    def pending(self) -> List[dict]:\n42\t        \"\"\"The phone-facing queue — minimal fields only (no subject leaks over the tunnel).\"\"\"\n43\t        from ..agents.approvals import pending\n44\t        items = pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=self._authorized())\n45\t        return [{\"seq\": r.seq, \"tier\": r.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": r.kind} for r in items]\n46\t\n47\t    def submit_device_approval(self, payload: dict) -> int:\n48\t        \"\"\"Append a DEVICE-signed approval the phone produced — ONLY if the signing device is\n49\t        currently authorized AND the signature verifies (fail-closed). A rogue/revoked device or a\n50\t        forged signature is refused; the signed target_seq binds (no replay).\"\"\"\n51\t        from ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n52\t        from ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n53\t\n54\t        class _Rec:\n55\t            def __init__(self, p): self.payload = p\n56\t        authorized = self._authorized()\n57\t        if payload.get(\"signal\") != APPROVAL_SIGNAL:\n58\t            raise ValueError(\"not an approval record\")\n59\t        if payload.get(\"pubkey\") not in authorized:\n60\t            raise ValueError(\"signing device is not authorized\")\n61\t        if not verify_approval(_Rec(payload), self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized):\n62\t            raise ValueError(\"approval signature invalid\")\n63\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n64\t                                 payload={**payload, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n65\t                                 supersedes_id=payload.get(\"target_seq\"))\n66\t\n67\t    def panic_engage(self, *, by: str = \"phone\") -> int:\n68\t        \"\"\"Halt the mesh from the phone. ANY engage halts (fail-safe) — no signature needed for the\n69\t        SAFE direction. Release stays owner-only at the desktop (the dangerous direction is signed).\"\"\"\n70\t        from ..governor.killswitch import SIGNAL as KS_SIGNAL\n71\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n72\t                                 payload={\"signal\": KS_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\", \"by\": by,\n73\t                                          \"reason\": \"panic halt from phone\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n74\t\n75\t    def relay(self, text: str) -> str:\n76\t        \"\"\"Relay a KERNEL command — the same T0-router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as voice/UI.\"\"\"\n77\t        from ..voice.dispatch import KernelDispatch\n78\t        return KernelDispatch().send(text)\n79\t"}]},"uuid":"5b59c890-1584-44df-9646-9e276309f9a9","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:07:59.681Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3436ec0e-d821-4fe4-abe7-b0b40e8bd66f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c565e687-bdd1-4b89-897d-f051ce6c97f4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JoC9nfv3ZS7bB7YQykyyJp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"PushNotifier (Phase 7, WS-D D-vi) — pushes to the phone when an A2/A3 item QUEUES, fed by the\n2\tSHARED P0 `SpineTailer` (the same primitive the UI SSE feed uses). The push carries ONLY\n3\t`{seq, tier, kind}` — never the subject, never the payload, never a secret — so nothing sensitive\n4\tcrosses even the WireGuard tunnel; the phone fetches any detail on demand over the daemon API. No\n5\tcloud push (that would egress the private subject off owned hardware).\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n11\tfrom ..spine.tail import SpineTailer\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tclass PushNotifier:\n15\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, since_seq: Optional[int] = None):\n16\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n17\t        start = since_seq if since_seq is not None else self.store.next_seq - 1\n18\t        self.tailer = SpineTailer(self.store, since_seq=start)\n19\t\n20\t    def poll(self) -> List[dict]:\n21\t        \"\"\"New pushes since the last poll: one per newly-QUEUED A2/A3 item, minimal fields only.\"\"\"\n22\t        out: List[dict] = []\n23\t        for ev in self.tailer.poll():\n24\t            rec = self.store.get(ev[\"seq\"])\n25\t            p = rec.payload if rec else {}\n26\t            if (p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == \"awaiting-approval\"\n27\t                    and p.get(\"tier\") in (\"A2\", \"A3\")):\n28\t                out.append({\"seq\": ev[\"seq\"], \"tier\": p.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": ev[\"kind\"]})   # no subject/secret\n29\t        return out\n30\t"}]},"uuid":"c6fa5441-519d-4a4e-a02d-36388948b301","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:00.158Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c565e687-bdd1-4b89-897d-f051ce6c97f4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c6fa5441-519d-4a4e-a02d-36388948b301","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAN3mHF6GRk7ConpW9F3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TukUpsDcpLmMC9LaAB3wHV","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/ui/server.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2831,"cache_read_input_tokens":12120,"output_tokens":575,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2831},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":575,"cache_read_input_tokens":12120,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2831,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2831,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAN3id15GzsCnag4pZFe","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"2bbd4081-ecf6-4675-beb7-6ee1ebd0e54b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:00.541Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"2bbd4081-ecf6-4675-beb7-6ee1ebd0e54b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TukUpsDcpLmMC9LaAB3wHV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL glass-cockpit server (Phase 7, WS-C) — a loopback-only, two-plane HTTP server (stdlib\n2\tonly, minimal auditable surface). Mirrors the MCP server's posture: read-only over the spine +\n3\tprovenance on every atom, plus a CSRF-proof owner-signed action plane.\n4\t\n5\tSecurity model (the red-pen keystone):\n6\t  • binds 127.0.0.1 ONLY (never 0.0.0.0/public).\n7\t  • a session TOKEN is minted at startup and PRINTED TO THE TERMINAL (so only someone at the machine\n8\t    can drive it, and no web page can read it). The served page embeds it; a cross-origin page cannot.\n9\t  • READ plane (GET /api/*): requires the token (header `X-SIGIL-Token`, or `?token=` for SSE which\n10\t    can't set headers). Read/query only — EXCEPT `/api/ask`, which DISPATCHES a WARDEN-gated KERNEL\n11\t    query (a subprocess), so it carries the FULL action gate (token + Origin + Host), not just token.\n12\t  • ACTION plane (POST /api/action): requires the token AND an EXACT-MATCH loopback `Origin`/`Referer`\n13\t    AND a `Host` in the allowlist (defeats DNS-rebinding). Routes ONLY the closed owner-signed action\n14\t    set. The private key never touches the browser — the server signs (see `ui.actions`).\n15\t  • Static assets + the index bootstrap are token-free (they carry no secret; the token is injected\n16\t    into the page as a data attribute, unreadable cross-origin). A strict CSP + external `self` JS/CSS\n17\t    keeps the page functional AND locked down.\"\"\"\n18\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n19\t\n20\timport hmac\n21\timport json\n22\timport time\n23\tfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\n24\tfrom pathlib import Path\n25\tfrom urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse\n26\t\n27\tfrom ..config import SPINE_PATH\n28\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n29\tfrom ..spine.tail import SpineTailer\n30\tfrom ..spine.verify import verify_record\n31\tfrom . import actions as _actions\n32\t\n33\t_STATIC = Path(__file__).parent / \"static\"\n34\t_CSP = \"default-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tclass UIServer(ThreadingHTTPServer):\n38\t    daemon_threads = True\n39\t\n40\t    def __init__(self, addr, handler, *, token: str, spine_path: Path):\n41\t        super().__init__(addr, handler)\n42\t        self.token = token\n43\t        self.spine_path = spine_path\n44\t        port = self.server_address[1]              # the ACTUAL bound port (correct even for port 0)\n45\t        self.allowed_hosts = frozenset({f\"127.0.0.1:{port}\", f\"localhost:{port}\"})\n46\t        self.allowed_origins = frozenset({f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}\", f\"http://localhost:{port}\"})\n47\t\n48\t    def store(self) -> SpineStore:\n49\t        return SpineStore(self.spine_path)         # fresh read each request (cheap, current)\n50\t\n51\t\n52\tdef _json_bytes(obj) -> bytes:\n53\t    return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False).encode(\"utf-8\")\n54\t\n55\t\n56\tclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n57\t    server_version = \"sigil-ui/1.0\"\n58\t    timeout = 30                        # per-connection socket timeout (BLOCK-4: no hung reader)\n59\t    _MAX_BODY = 65536                   # action bodies are tiny; cap to avoid a Content-Length hang\n60\t    _STATIC_OK = frozenset({\"app.js\", \"style.css\"})\n61\t\n62\t    # never log the token (it can ride in ?token= for SSE)\n63\t    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):  # noqa: A003\n64\t        pass\n65\t\n66\t    # --- auth -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n67\t    def _query(self) -> dict:\n68\t        return parse_qs(urlparse(self.path).query)\n69\t\n70\t    def _token_ok(self) -> bool:\n71\t        q = self._query()\n72\t        tok = self.headers.get(\"X-SIGIL-Token\") or (q.get(\"token\", [\"\"])[0])\n73\t        return bool(tok) and hmac.compare_digest(tok, self.server.token)\n74\t\n75\t    def _action_ok(self) -> bool:\n76\t        if self.headers.get(\"Host\", \"\") not in self.server.allowed_hosts:\n77\t            return False                                       # anti DNS-rebinding\n78\t        o = self.headers.get(\"Origin\") or \"\"\n79\t        ref = self.headers.get(\"Referer\") or \"\"\n80\t        # EXACT-match the Origin (a `startswith` lets `http://127.0.0.1:80.evil.com` slip); a Referer,\n81\t        # if present, must sit under an allowed origin. A cross-origin Origin → refuse.\n82\t        if o and o not in self.server.allowed_origins:\n83\t            return False\n84\t        if ref and not any(ref.startswith(a + \"/\") or ref == a for a in self.server.allowed_origins):\n85\t            return False\n86\t        return self._token_ok()\n87\t\n88\t    # --- response helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n89\t    def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype=\"application/json\"):\n90\t        self.send_response(code)\n91\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", ctype)\n92\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Length\", str(len(body)))\n93\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Security-Policy\", _CSP)\n94\t        self.send_header(\"X-Content-Type-Options\", \"nosniff\")\n95\t        self.send_header(\"Referrer-Policy\", \"no-referrer\")\n96\t        self.end_headers()\n97\t        try:\n98\t            self.wfile.write(body)\n99\t        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):\n100\t            pass\n101\t\n102\t    def _json(self, obj, code=200):\n103\t        self._send(code, _json_bytes(obj))\n104\t\n105\t    def _deny(self, code=403, msg=\"forbidden\"):\n106\t        self._json({\"error\": msg}, code)\n107\t\n108\t    # --- GET (read plane) -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n109\t    def do_GET(self):\n110\t        path = urlparse(self.path).path\n111\t        if path in (\"/\", \"/index.html\"):\n112\t            return self._serve_index()\n113\t        if path.startswith(\"/static/\"):\n114\t            return self._serve_static(path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1])   # token-free bootstrap assets (no secret)\n115\t        if not path.startswith(\"/api/\"):\n116\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n117\t        if not self._token_ok():\n118\t            return self._deny(401, \"missing/invalid token\")\n119\t        if path == \"/api/ask\":\n120\t            # /api/ask DISPATCHES a KERNEL subprocess → it gets the FULL action gate, not just token.\n121\t            return self._ask(self._query().get(\"q\", [\"\"])[0]) if self._action_ok() else self._deny(403, \"denied\")\n122\t        if path == \"/api/snapshot\":\n123\t            from ..dashboard import snapshot\n124\t            return self._json(snapshot(self.server.store()))\n125\t        if path.startswith(\"/api/record/\"):\n126\t            return self._record(path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1])\n127\t        if path == \"/api/stream\":\n128\t            return self._sse()\n129\t        if path == \"/api/graph\":\n130\t            return self._graph()\n131\t        if path == \"/api/graph/entity\":\n132\t            return self._graph_entity(self._query().get(\"name\", [\"\"])[0])\n133\t        if path == \"/api/classify\":\n134\t            return self._classify(self._query().get(\"tool\", [\"\"])[0])\n135\t        return self._deny(404, \"unknown endpoint\")\n136\t\n137\t    def _serve_static(self, name):\n138\t        if name not in self._STATIC_OK:\n139\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n140\t        try:\n141\t            data = (_STATIC / name).read_bytes()\n142\t        except OSError:\n143\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n144\t        ctype = \"application/javascript\" if name.endswith(\".js\") else \"text/css\"\n145\t        self._send(200, data, ctype=f\"{ctype}; charset=utf-8\")\n146\t\n147\t    def _serve_index(self):\n148\t        try:\n149\t            html = (_STATIC / \"index.html\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n150\t        except OSError:\n151\t            return self._deny(500, \"ui missing\")\n152\t        html = html.replace(\"__SIGIL_TOKEN__\", self.server.token)   # embed token for the same-origin page\n153\t        self._send(200, html.encode(\"utf-8\"), ctype=\"text/html; charset=utf-8\")\n154\t\n155\t    def _record(self, raw):\n156\t        try:\n157\t            seq = int(raw)\n158\t        except ValueError:\n159\t            return self._deny(400, \"bad seq\")\n160\t        rec = self.server.store().get(seq)\n161\t        if rec is None:\n162\t            return self._json({\"error\": \"no such record\", \"seq\": seq, \"note\": \"no grounded record — not fabricated\"}, 404)\n163\t        ok, reason = verify_record(rec)                          # re-verify the atom LIVE (prove-don't-guess)\n164\t        self._json({\"seq\": rec.seq, \"kind\": rec.kind, \"source\": rec.source, \"actor\": rec.actor,\n165\t                    \"ts\": rec.ts, \"entry_hash\": rec.entry_hash, \"prev_hash\": rec.prev_hash,\n166\t                    \"payload\": rec.payload, \"integrity_ok\": ok, \"integrity_reason\": reason})\n167\t\n168\t    def _graph(self):\n169\t        try:\n170\t            from ..graph import health\n171\t            self._json({\"health\": health()})\n172\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — graph may not be built yet\n173\t            self._json({\"error\": \"graph unavailable\", \"note\": str(e)[:200]})\n174\t\n175\t    def _graph_entity(self, name):\n176\t        try:\n177\t            from ..graph import entity\n178\t            self._json(entity(name))\n179\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n180\t            self._json({\"error\": \"graph unavailable\", \"note\": str(e)[:200]})\n181\t\n182\t    def _classify(self, tool):\n183\t        from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n184\t        self._json({\"tool\": tool, \"tier\": KernelClassifier().classify(tool).label()})\n185\t\n186\t    def _ask(self, q):\n187\t        from ..voice.dispatch import KernelDispatch\n188\t        self._json({\"q\": q, \"answer\": KernelDispatch().send(q)})\n189\t\n190\t    def _sse(self):\n191\t        self.send_response(200)\n192\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"text/event-stream\")\n193\t        self.send_header(\"Cache-Control\", \"no-cache\")\n194\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Security-Policy\", _CSP)\n195\t        self.end_headers()\n196\t        try:\n197\t            since = int(self._query().get(\"since\", [\"-1\"])[0])\n198\t        except (ValueError, TypeError):\n199\t            since = -1                                          # any malformed cursor → from genesis\n200\t        tailer = SpineTailer(self.server.store(), since_seq=since)\n201\t        try:\n202\t            while True:\n203\t                sent = False\n204\t                for ev in tailer.poll():\n205\t                    self.wfile.write(f\"data: {json.dumps(ev, ensure_ascii=False)}\\n\\n\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n206\t                    sent = True\n207\t                self.wfile.write(b\": hb\\n\\n\")                    # heartbeat / flush\n208\t                self.wfile.flush()\n209\t                if not sent:\n210\t                    time.sleep(0.25)\n211\t        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError, OSError):\n212\t            return                                               # client closed — end the stream\n213\t\n214\t    # --- POST (action plane) ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n215\t    def do_POST(self):\n216\t        path = urlparse(self.path).path\n217\t        if path != \"/api/action\":\n218\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n219\t        if not self._action_ok():\n220\t            return self._deny(403, \"action denied (token / origin / host)\")\n221\t        try:\n222\t            length = int(self.headers.get(\"Content-Length\", \"0\"))\n223\t            if length > self._MAX_BODY:                         # BLOCK-4: cap the body (no CL hang / alloc)\n224\t                return self._deny(413, \"body too large\")\n225\t            body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length) or b\"{}\")\n226\t            action = str(body.get(\"action\", \"\"))\n227\t            result = _actions.do_action(action, body, store=self.server.store())\n228\t            self._json(result)\n229\t        except (ValueError, KeyError) as e:\n230\t            self._deny(400, f\"bad request: {e}\")\n231\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — ApprovalError etc. → 400, never 500-leak internals\n232\t            self._deny(400, f\"action failed: {str(e)[:200]}\")\n233\t\n234\t\n235\tdef build_server(*, token: str, port: int = 8733, spine_path=None) -> UIServer:\n236\t    return UIServer((\"127.0.0.1\", port), Handler, token=token,\n237\t                    spine_path=Path(spine_path) if spine_path else SPINE_PATH)\n238\t\n239\t\n240\tdef serve(*, token: str, port: int = 8733, spine_path=None) -> None:\n241\t    srv = build_server(token=token, port=port, spine_path=spine_path)\n242\t    print(f\"  SIGIL cockpit → http://127.0.0.1:{port}/?token={token}\")\n243\t    print(\"  (loopback only; the token gates every request — keep it to yourself)\")\n244\t    try:\n245\t        srv.serve_forever()\n246\t    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n247\t        srv.shutdown()\n248\t"}]},"uuid":"c77f7acc-bfdb-451c-b21b-69004022140c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:00.558Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2bbd4081-ecf6-4675-beb7-6ee1ebd0e54b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"26a688ac-fa4c-4dd7-ae67-6135302c11c9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PquPmMFkzuHBu3nD6e74XA","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\",\n27\t             \"has_hid_inject\", \"has_camera_stream\")\n28\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n32\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n33\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n34\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n35\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"]),\n36\t            \"has_hid_inject\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_hid_inject\", False)),\n37\t            \"has_camera_stream\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_camera_stream\", False))}\n38\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n39\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n43\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n44\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n45\t    latest: dict = {}\n46\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n47\t        p = r.payload\n48\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n49\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n50\t    return latest\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n54\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n55\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n56\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n57\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n61\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n62\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n63\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n67\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n68\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n69\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n70\t    state: dict = {}\n71\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n72\t        p = r.payload\n73\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n74\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n75\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n76\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tclass DeviceApprover:\n80\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n81\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n82\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n83\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n84\t        self.store = store\n85\t        self.device_key = device_key\n86\t\n87\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n88\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n89\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n90\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n91\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n92\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n93\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"67bf2347-1e66-44a9-af65-a6ffdefe9c9e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:06.349Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"26a688ac-fa4c-4dd7-ae67-6135302c11c9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"67bf2347-1e66-44a9-af65-a6ffdefe9c9e","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAN4W6TuSEYDGbHfGqBR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01B7S4DFEddWbZSsMEjXMhPR","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/approvals.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7959,"cache_read_input_tokens":14951,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7959,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAN4UYwhdSBNBLobprxo","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"981dc5eb-d587-42b9-b3e1-a0095471abcb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:07.104Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"981dc5eb-d587-42b9-b3e1-a0095471abcb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01B7S4DFEddWbZSsMEjXMhPR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"95dc678b-8839-4d75-abdb-3059654a63ff","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:07.127Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"981dc5eb-d587-42b9-b3e1-a0095471abcb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c4cfde2d-1884-4e03-bfc6-a3636cc253eb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UDDywKBEVREwrXEASxFhHk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The owner-signed action broker (Phase 7, WS-C C-v) — the single funnel for every gated action the\n2\tUI (and later the mobile bridge) can trigger. The browser never holds key material: it sends an\n3\tauthenticated REQUEST (\"approve seq N\"), and the SERVER signs with the persisted owner key exactly\n4\tas `cli.cmd_warden`/`cmd_approve` do. No new authority path — this only calls the existing\n5\towner-signed cores (`ApprovalQueue`, `KillSwitch`, `PromotionPolicy`).\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import Optional\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n11\t\n12\t# the closed set of gated actions the plane will route (fail-closed: anything else is refused)\n13\tACTIONS = frozenset({\"approve\", \"deny\", \"kill\", \"release\", \"promote\", \"revoke\"})\n14\t\n15\t\n16\tdef do_action(action: str, params: dict, *, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None) -> dict:\n17\t    \"\"\"Perform one owner-signed action. Returns {seq, action, ...} or raises ValueError/ApprovalError.\n18\t    The owner key is the persisted identity (auto-created once) — never supplied by the caller.\"\"\"\n19\t    if action not in ACTIONS:\n20\t        raise ValueError(f\"unknown action: {action!r}\")\n21\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n22\t    from ..agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\n23\t    from ..governor import KillSwitch, PromotionPolicy\n24\t    from ..governor.identity import ensure_owner_keypair\n25\t    owner = ensure_owner_keypair()\n26\t    reason = str(params.get(\"reason\", \"\"))[:200]\n27\t\n28\t    if action in (\"approve\", \"deny\"):\n29\t        seq = int(params[\"seq\"])\n30\t        q = ApprovalQueue(store, owner_key=owner)\n31\t        out = q.approve(seq, reason=reason) if action == \"approve\" else q.deny(seq, reason=reason)\n32\t        return {\"ok\": True, \"action\": action, \"target_seq\": seq, \"recorded_seq\": out}\n33\t    if action in (\"kill\", \"release\"):\n34\t        ks = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=owner)\n35\t        out = ks.engage(reason=reason) if action == \"kill\" else ks.release(reason=reason)\n36\t        return {\"ok\": True, \"action\": action, \"recorded_seq\": out}\n37\t    if action in (\"promote\", \"revoke\"):\n38\t        pp = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=owner)\n39\t        agent, scope = str(params[\"agent\"]), str(params.get(\"scope\", \"*\"))\n40\t        out = pp.grant(agent, scope) if action == \"promote\" else pp.revoke(agent, scope)\n41\t        return {\"ok\": out is not None, \"action\": action, \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope, \"recorded_seq\": out}\n42\t    raise ValueError(f\"unhandled action: {action!r}\")   # unreachable (ACTIONS-gated)\n43\t"}]},"uuid":"749362cc-539c-4a28-aa70-4f25ee34f975","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:07.667Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c4cfde2d-1884-4e03-bfc6-a3636cc253eb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

cursor.get(key, 0)\n32\t            events, seen = ingest_transcript(store, sf, proj.name, skip_records=skip, max_events=a.max_events)\n33\t            cursor[key] = seen\n34\t            if                    max_events=a.max_events)\n41\t                if sub:\n42\t                    print(f\"  +{sub:>5} subagent events  {proj.name}/{sf.stem[:12]}…/subagents\")\n43\t                    total_events += sub\n44\t            if a.max_events and total_events >= a.max_events:\n45\t                break\n46\t        if a.max_events and total_events >= a.max_events:\n47\t            break\n48\t    if a.docs:\n49\t        d = ingest_docs(store)\n50\t        print(f\"  +{d} document chunks (curated memory/*.md)\")\n51\t        total_events += d\n52\t    if a.git or git_only:\n53\t        from .ingest.git import ingest_git\n54\t        g = ingest_git(store, cursor=cursor)  # same cursor dict → saved once below (no clobber)\n55\t        print(f\"  +{g} commit events (git history)\")\n56\t        total_events += g\n57\t    cur.save(cursor)\n58\t    print(f\"ingested {total_events} new events; spine now holds {store.count()} records\")\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef cmd_index(a) -> None:\n62\t    store, vi = SpineStore(), VectorIndex()\n63\t    since = vi.last_indexed_seq()\n64\t    print(f\"  indexing spine records above seq {since} ...\")\n65\t    n = vi.index_spine(store, since_seq=since)\n66\t    print(f\"indexed {n} new records; vector collection now {vi.count()} points\")\n67\t\n68\t\n69\tdef cmd_sign(a) -> None:\n70\t    head = checkpoint()\n71\t    print(f\"signed (anti-rollback).\n249\t    AUTHENTICATED: the (count, head_hash, pubkey) tuple must carry a valid Ed25519 signature by\n250\t    the WARDEN key `pubkey`, so a rogue local caller cannot POISON the high-water (e.g. set a\n251\t    huge count to make every future verify fail). Only the KERNEL, which holds the WARDEN private\n252\t    key, can anchor.\"\"\"\n253\t    if not _verify_ed25519(_warden_anchor_msg(int(a.count), a.head_hash, a.pubkey), a.sig, a.pubkey):\n254\t        print(\"REJECTED: warden-anchor-set signature invalid (only the WARDEN key may anchor)\", file=sys.stderr)\n255\t        sys.exit(2)\n256\t    store = SpineStore()\n257\t    store.append(kind=\"warden_checkpoint\", source=\"warden\", actor=\"warden\",\n258\t                 payload={\"count\": int(a.count), \"head_hash\": a.head_hash, \"pubkey\": a.pubkey})\n259\t    checkpoint(store)  # re-sign the spine head so the anchor sits under the spine's signature (anti-truncation)\n260\t    print(f\"anchored WARDEN head into spine: count={a.count} head={a.head_hash[:16]}\")\n261\t\n262\t\n263\tdef cmd_warden_anchor_get(a) -> None:\n264\t    \"\"\"Print the highest WARDEN head ever anchored FOR THIS KEY, as JSON {count, head_hash}.\n265\t    FAILS CLOSED (exit 2) if the spine's own integrity does not hold — otherwise a surgical edit\n266\t    to a warden_checkpoint line, or a spine tail-truncation removing recent anchors, would silently\n267\t    lower the high-water and let a rolled-back WARDEN log pass verify. Scoped by pubkey so a key\n268\t    rotation starts a fresh lineage.\"\"\"\n269\t    import json as _json\n270\t    store = SpineStore()\n271\t    ok, msg = store.verify()                       # chain: catches a tampered/deleted anchor record\n272\t    if not ok:\n273\t        print(f\"REJECTED: spine integrity failed — {msg}\", file=sys.stderr)\n274\t        sys.exit(2)\n275\t    hok, hmsg = verify_checkpoint(store)           # signed head: catches spine tail-truncation\n276\t    if not hok and \"TAMPER\" in hmsg.upper():\n277\t        print(f\"REJECTED: spine head tampered — {hmsg}\", file=sys.stderr)\n278\t        sys.exit(2)\n279\t    best_count, best_hash = 0, \"\"\n280\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n281\t        if r.kind == \"warden_checkpoint\" and r.payload.get(\"pubkey\") == a.pubkey:\n282\t            c = int(r.payload.get(\"count\", 0))\n283\t            if c >= best_count:\n284\t                best_count, best_hash = c, r.payload.get(\"head_hash\", \"\")\n285\t    print(_json.dumps({\"count\": best_count, \"head_hash\": best_hash}))\n286\t\n287\t\n288\tdef cmd_warden(a) -> None:\n289\t    \"\"\"Phase 6 governor controls (SIGIL §5): kill switch + per-kind promotion policy. Governance\n290\t    mutations are signed by the persisted OWNER key (auto-created once if absent).\"\"\"\n291\t    from .governor import KillSwitch, PromotionPolicy\n292\t    from .governor.identity import ensure_owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n293\t    store = SpineStore()\n294\t    if a.action == \"status\":\n295\t        print(f\"  kill switch: {'ENGAGED (mesh halted)' if KillSwitch(store).is_engaged() else 'released (mesh live)'}\")\n296\t        return\n297\t    ok = ensure_owner_keypair()   # owner signing key (the trust anchor)\n298\t    if a.action == \"kill\":\n299\t        seq = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok).engage(reason=a.reason or \"\")\n300\t        print(f\"  KILL SWITCH ENGAGED (seq {seq}) — agent mesh halted; perception + memory read stay alive\")\n301\t    elif a.action == \"release\":\n302\t        seq = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok).release(reason=a.reason or \"\")\n303\t        print(f\"  kill switch released (seq {seq}, owner-signed) — agent mesh live again\")\n304\t    elif a.action == \"promote\":\n305\t        seq = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok).grant(a.agent, a.scope or \"*\")\n306\t        print(f\"  refused: {a.agent} has no promotion path (SIGIL §4.6)\" if seq is None\n307\t              else f\"  promoted {a.agent}/{a.scope or '*'} → A2 auto-approve, owner-signed (seq {seq})\")\n308\t    elif a.action == \"revoke\":\n309\t        seq = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok).revoke(a.agent, a.scope or \"*\")\n310\t        print(f\"  revoked promotion for {a.agent}/{a.scope or '*'} (seq {seq})\")\n311\t\n312\t\n313\tdef cmd_audit(a) -> None:\n314\t    from .audit import render_audit, self_audit\n315\t    store = SpineStore()\n316\t    rows = self_audit(store, agent=a.agent)\n317\t    print(render_audit(rows, agent=a.agent))\n318\t\n319\t\n320\tdef cmd_approve(a) -> None:\n321\t    from .agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\n322\t    from .governor.identity import ensure_owner_keypair\n323\t    owner_key = ensure_owner_keypair()   # the persisted owner key IS the trusted signer\n324\t    q = ApprovalQueue(SpineStore(), owner_key=owner_key)\n325\t    try:\n326\t        fn = q.approve if a.decision == \"approve\" else q.deny\n327\t        seq = fn(a.seq, approver=a.approver or \"owner\", reason=a.reason or \"\")\n328\t        print(f\"  {a.decision}d queued seq {a.seq} → recorded at seq {seq}  (Ed25519 owner-signed)\")\n329\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n330\t        print(f\"  cannot {a.decision} seq {a.seq}: {e}\")\n331\t\n332\t\n333\tdef cmd_dashboard(a) -> None:\n334\t    from .dashboard import render_dashboard, snapshot\n335\t    print(render_dashboard(snapshot(SpineStore())))\n336\t\n337\t\n338\tdef cmd_scrape(a) -> None:\n339\t    \"\"\"SCRIBE grounded web research — crawl PUBLIC, scope-gated, robots-respecting, serve-the-quote.\"\"\"\n340\t    from .scrape import ScrapeScope\n341\t    from .scrape.researcher import WebResearcher\n342\t    from .spine.store import SpineStore\n343\t    if not a.scope_domain:\n344\t        print(\"  --scope-domain is required (deny-all by default): e.g. --scope-domain example.com\")\n345\t        return\n346\t    scope = ScrapeScope(a.scope_domain, include_subdomains=a.subdomains)\n347\t    store = SpineStore()\n348\t    res = WebResearcher(store).research_web(a.question or \"\", a.seed or [], scope)\n349\t    print(f\"  {'; '.join(res.notes)}\")\n350\t    for seq in res.applied:\n351\t        rec = store.get(seq)\n352\t        if rec and rec.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"web.research\":\n353\t            print(\"\\n\" + rec.payload.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n354\t\n355\t\n356\tdef cmd_host(a) -> None:\n357\t    \"\"\"Show this host's capability descriptor (WS-D) — what the mesh routes on.\"\"\"\n358\t    from .platform import host\n359\t    d = host().capabilities()\n360\t    print(f\"  host {d.host_id} · {d.os}\")\n361\t    print(f\"    screen={d.has_screen} camera={d.has_camera} gpu_vlm={d.has_gpu_vlm} always_on={d.always_on}\")\n362\t\n363\t\n364\tdef cmd_serve(a) -> None:\n365\t    \"\"\"Start the loopback glass-cockpit UI. SIGIL\n4\timports NO `framework.*` module at all, which is the strongest form of the sovereignty\n5\tdoctrine (§1.3 / §12): the offensive engine cannot even be loaded into a SIGIL process.\n6\t`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport sys\n11\t\n12\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, digest_payload, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n13\tfrom .chain import append_entry, build_chain, sign_head, verify_chain, verify_head\n14\tfrom .crypto import generate_keypair, sign, verify_one, verify_threshold, KeyPair\n15\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n16\t\n17\t__all__ = [\n18\t    \"canonical_json\", \"digest_payload\", \"evidence_signing_bytes\", \"sha256_hex\",\n19\t    \"append_entry\", \"build_chain\", \"sign_head\", \"verify_chain\", \"verify_head\",\n20\t    \"generate_keypair\", \"sign\", \"verify_one\", \"verify_threshold\", \"KeyPair\",\n21\t    \"AuthorizerKey\", \"ChainEntry\", \"Signature\", \"SignedChainHead\", \"TrustRoot\",\n22\t    \"assert_no_offense\",\n23\t]\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef assert_no_offense() -> None:\n27\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed: NO framework.* module may be loaded in a SIGIL process (doctrine §12).\"\"\"\n28\t    leaked = [m for m in sys.modules if m == \"framework\" or m.startswith(\"framework.\")]\n29\t    if leaked:\n30\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"SIGIL sovereignty violation: engine modules loaded: {leaked}\")\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"497bf853-c968-4103-960a-cf7ead60dec4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:14.934Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f9bc8eff-e49f-41bb-9ea3-c68e00a6daff","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Behaviour is byte-identical.\n6\t\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport hashlib\n10\timport json\n11\tfrom typing import Any, Final\n12\t\n13\t# Never change without a schema bump + migration (invalidates all existing signatures).\n14\t_EVIDENCE_DOMAIN: Final[bytes] = b\"crucible-evidence-v1\\x00\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef canonical_json(payload: Any) -> bytes:\n18\t    \"\"\"Deterministic UTF-8 JSON: sorted keys (recursive), compact separators.\"\"\"\n19\t    return json.dumps(\n20\t        payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), ensure_ascii=False,\n21\t    ).encode(\"utf-8\")\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tdef sha256_hex(data: bytes) -> str:\n25\t    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef digest_payload(payload: Any) -> str:\n29\t    \"\"\"sha256 of a JSON-serialisable payload in canonical form.\"\"\"\n30\t    return sha256_hex(canonical_json(payload))\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef evidence_signing_bytes(certificate_payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:\n34\t    \"\"\"The exact bytes signed/verified (canonical JSON under the domain tag).\"\"\"\n35\t    return _EVIDENCE_DOMAIN + canonical_json(certificate_payload)\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"2a0909b4-e9e7-486d-a209-98441706a4e2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:15.453Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3b2dd917-6898-420d-9d12-6273dafcd9f0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"74c0ed9b-fa79-4e00-bd60-204f0ece0f81","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KQAKfzQ8RwU28LA1Ds3kKN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Ed25519 primitives + m-of-n threshold verify.\n2\t\n3\tVENDORED VERBATIM from CRUCIBLE `framework/v2/entitlement/crypto.py` (owner's own work).\n4\t\"We do not roll our own crypto\" — Ed25519 is pyca `cryptography`. Signing helpers are\n5\tprovisioning-only; the runtime only ever verifies. The sole change from the source is a\n6\tlocal `IntegrityError` in place of the framework's `EntitlementError` (decoupling).\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport base64\n11\timport binascii\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\t\n14\tfrom cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature\n15\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (\n16\t    Ed25519PrivateKey,\n17\t    Ed25519PublicKey,\n18\t)\n19\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.serialization import (\n20\t    Encoding,\n21\t    NoEncryption,\n22\t    PrivateFormat,\n23\t    PublicFormat,\n24\t)\n25\t\n26\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, Signature, TrustRoot\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass IntegrityError(Exception):\n30\t    \"\"\"Malformed key/signature material (a provisioning error, not an attacker path).\"\"\"\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _b64decode_exact(value: str, expected_len: int, what: str) -> bytes:\n34\t    try:\n35\t        raw = base64.b64decode(value, validate=True)\n36\t    except (ValueError, binascii.Error) as e:\n37\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} is not valid base64: {e}\") from e\n38\t    if len(raw) != expected_len:\n39\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} decodes to {len(raw)} bytes, expected {expected_len}\")\n40\t    return raw\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef load_public_key(public_key_b64: str) -> Ed25519PublicKey:\n44\t    return Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(_b64decode_exact(public_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 public key\"))\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef verify_one(public_key_b64: str, message: bytes, signature_b64: str) -> bool:\n48\t    \"\"\"True iff a valid Ed25519 signature; False on bad sig; raises only on malformed material.\"\"\"\n49\t    pub = load_public_key(public_key_b64)\n50\t    sig = _b64decode_exact(signature_b64, 64, \"Ed25519 signature\")\n51\t    try:\n52\t        pub.verify(sig, message)\n53\t        return True\n54\t    except InvalidSignature:\n55\t        return False\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n59\tclass ThresholdResult:\n60\t    satisfied: bool\n61\t    valid_signers: tuple[str, ...]\n62\t    threshold: int\n63\t    reason: str\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef verify_threshold(message: bytes, signatures: list[Signature], trust_root: TrustRoot) -> ThresholdResult:\n67\t    \"\"\"Count distinct trust-root authorisers with a valid signature; compare to threshold.\"\"\"\n68\t    by_id: dict[str, AuthorizerKey] = {a.key_id: a for a in trust_root.authorizers}\n69\t    valid: list[str] = []\n70\t    seen: set[str] = set()\n71\t    for sig in signatures:\n72\t        if sig.key_id in seen:\n73\t            continue\n74\t        seen.add(sig.key_id)\n75\t        authorizer = by_id.get(sig.key_id)\n76\t        if authorizer is None:\n77\t            continue\n78\t        if verify_one(authorizer.public_key_b64, message, sig.signature_b64):\n79\t            valid.append(sig.key_id)\n80\t    satisfied = len(valid) >= trust_root.threshold\n81\t    reason = (\n82\t        f\"{len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) >= threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n83\t        if satisfied else\n84\t        f\"only {len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) < threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n85\t    )\n86\t    return ThresholdResult(satisfied=satisfied, valid_signers=tuple(valid), threshold=trust_root.threshold, reason=reason)\n87\t\n88\t\n89\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n90\tclass KeyPair:\n91\t    public_key_b64: str\n92\t    private_key_b64: str\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tdef generate_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n96\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()\n97\t    raw_priv = priv.private_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PrivateFormat.Raw, NoEncryption())\n98\t    raw_pub = priv.public_key().public_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PublicFormat.Raw)\n99\t    return KeyPair(\n100\t        public_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_pub).decode(\"ascii\"),\n101\t        private_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_priv).decode(\"ascii\"),\n102\t    )\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tdef sign(private_key_b64: str, message: bytes) -> str:\n106\t    raw = _b64decode_exact(private_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 private key\")\n107\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(raw)\n108\t    return base64.b64encode(priv.sign(message)).decode(\"ascii\")\n109\t"}]},"uuid":"2943df84-2cfb-4880-afd5-3e9844a40d3e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:16.427Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"74c0ed9b-fa79-4e00-bd60-204f0ece0f81","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Host\n2\tcapabilities and phone/device authorizations are owner-SIGNED spine records, so the mesh topology\n3\tis itself provable, tamper-evident memory; compute (vision/VLM) can run on the best-capable online\n4\thost, but authority still flows through the one signed spine + WARDEN. Offense-free.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n6\t\n7\tassert_no_offense()\n8\t\n9\tfrom .registry import (  # noqa: E402\n10\t    DeviceApprover,\n11\t    advertise_capability,\n12\t    authorize_device,\n13\t    authorized_devices,\n14\t    capability_map,\n15\t    revoke_device,\n16\t)\n17\t\n18\t__all__ = [\"advertise_capability\", \"capability_map\", \"authorize_device\", \"revoke_device\",\n19\t           \"authorized_devices\", \"DeviceApprover\"]\n20\t"}]},"uuid":"2732e6ec-13b2-4ff6-a02c-9ad994c016c7","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:16.655Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8a0140fa-4663-4952-90e9-b61e8f3e866e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
