{"parentUuid":"2dcbcdcd-9a52-4437-bbd7-95858588421a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017jGSXwfk4ZAfLgnRKUGwq9","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Owner identity for governance authentication (Phase 6). The trust anchor is the SAME solo-owner\n2\tEd25519 keypair the spine checkpoint already persists (`KEYS_DIR/owner.priv|pub`, the 1-of-1 trust\n3\troot) — governance events (promotion grants, kill release, approvals) are signed by it and verified\n4\tagainst it. Reads are fail-closed: a missing key returns None, so nothing forged is ever trusted.\n5\t\n6\t`owner_pubkey()`/`owner_keypair()` READ the anchor (never generate — verification must not mint\n7\ttrust). `ensure_owner_keypair()` is for owner-initiated SIGNING actions (CLI engage/grant/approve):\n8\tit generates+persists the anchor once if absent, mirroring the checkpoint's `_owner_keys()`.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport os\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom ..config import KEYS_DIR\n15\tfrom ..reuse import KeyPair, generate_keypair\n16\t\n17\t_PRIV = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.priv\"\n18\t_PUB = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.pub\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef owner_pubkey() -> Optional[str]:\n22\t    try:\n23\t        return (_PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip() or None)\n24\t    except OSError:\n25\t        return None\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef owner_keypair() -> Optional[KeyPair]:\n29\t    try:\n30\t        priv = _PRIV.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n31\t        pub = _PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n32\t    except OSError:\n33\t        return None\n34\t    return KeyPair(public_key_b64=pub, private_key_b64=priv) if priv and pub else None\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef ensure_owner_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n38\t    \"\"\"Return the persisted owner keypair, generating+persisting it once if absent (owner signing\n39\t    path only). Same key material the checkpoint uses, so governance and the spine head share one\n40\t    owner identity.\"\"\"\n41\t    kp = owner_keypair()\n42\t    if kp is not None:\n43\t        return kp\n44\t    KEYS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n45\t    kp = generate_keypair()\n46\t    _PRIV.write_text(kp.private_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n47\t    _PUB.write_text(kp.public_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n48\t    for f in (_PRIV,):\n49\t        try:\n50\t            os.chmod(f, 0o600)\n51\t        except OSError:\n52\t            pass\n53\t    return kp\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"42629ea1-95fb-4004-9455-dcd031384c67","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:16.859Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2dcbcdcd-9a52-4437-bbd7-95858588421a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Path\n10\t\n11\tfrom sigil.agents.base import Agent, Proposal, Tier\n12\tfrom sigil.governor import Governor\n13\tfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\n14\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n15\tfrom sigil.ui.server import build_server\n16\t\n17\tOWNER = generate_keypair()\n18\tOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n19\tTOKEN = \"test-secret-token-xyz\"\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass _Emitter(Agent):\n23\t    name = \"TESTER\"\n24\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n25\t\n26\t    def __init__(self, store):\n27\t        super().__init__(store, governor=Governor(store, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey=OP))\n28\t\n29\t    def run(self, tier, kind=\"draft\"):\n30\t        return self._dispatch([Proposal(kind, {\"subject\": \"please approve me\"}, tier)])\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _spine():\n34\t    p = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\n35\t    SpineStore(p).append(kind=\"message\", source=\"x\", actor=\"user\", payload={\"text\": \"hello world\"})\n36\t    return p\n37\t\n38\t\n39\tdef _serve(spine_path):\n40\t    srv = build_server(token=TOKEN, port=0, spine_path=spine_path)   # port 0 = ephemeral\n41\t    port = srv.server_address[1]\n42\t    threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()\n43\t    time.sleep(0.05)\n44\t    return srv, port\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef _get(port, path, *, token=TOKEN, headers=None):\n48\t    h = dict(headers or {})\n49\t    if token is not None:\n50\t        h[\"X-SIGIL-Token\"] = token\n51\t    req = urllib.request.Request(f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}{path}\", headers=h)\n52\t    try:\n53\t        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as r:\n54\t            return r.status, r.read().decode()\n55\t    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n56\t        return e.code, e.read().decode()\n57\t\n58\t\n59\tdef _post(port, path, body, *, token=TOKEN, headers=None):\n60\t    h = {\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", **(headers or {})}\n61\t    if token is not None:\n62\t        h[\"X-SIGIL-Token\"] = token\n63\t    req = urllib.request.Request(f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}{path}\", data=json.dumps(body).encode(),\n64\t                                 headers=h, method=\"POST\")\n65\t    try:\n66\t        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as r:\n67\t            return r.status, r.read().decode()\n68\t    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n69\t        return e.code, e.read().decode()\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tdef test_binds_loopback_only():\n73\t    srv, _ = _serve(_spine())\n74\t    assert srv.server_address[0] == \"127.0.0.1\", \"the cockpit binds 127.0.0.1 only, never 0.0.0.0\"\n75\t    srv.shutdown()\n76\t\n77\t\n78\tdef test_read_plane_requires_the_token():\n79\t    srv, port = _serve(_spine())\n80\t    try:\n81\t        code, _ = _get(port, \"/api/snapshot\", token=None)\n82\t        assert code == 401, \"no token → 401\"\n83\t        code, body = _get(port, \"/api/snapshot\")\n84\t        assert code == 200 and \"head_seq\" in body, \"with the token → the snapshot\"\n85\t        code, _ = _get(port, \"/api/snapshot\", token=\"WRONG\")\n86\t        assert code == 401, \"a wrong token → 401\"\n87\t    finally:\n88\t        srv.shutdown()\n89\t\n90\t\n91\tdef test_record_endpoint_reverifies_provenance():\n92\t    p = _spine()\n93\t    srv, port = _serve(p)\n94\t    try:\n95\t        code, body = _get(port, \"/api/record/0\")\n96\t        d = json.loads(body)\n97\t        assert code == 200 and d[\"integrity_ok\"] is True and d[\"entry_hash\"], \"an atom re-verifies live\"\n98\t        # tamper the record on disk → the endpoint reports integrity broken, not truth\n99\t        lines = Path(p).read_text().splitlines()\n100\t        rec = json.loads(lines[0]); rec[\"payload\"] = {\"text\": \"TAMPERED\"}; lines[0] = json.dumps(rec)\n101\t        Path(p).write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n102\t        code, body = _get(port, \"/api/record/0\")\n103\t        assert json.loads(body)[\"integrity_ok\"] is False, \"a tampered atom is flagged, never served as verified\"\n104\t        code, body = _get(port, \"/api/record/999\")\n105\t        assert code == 404 and {"parentUuid":"f2d6975a-92e1-480a-9e49-afff91ce7f26","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012dXPYkZyzPucTf6sSs6B71","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use strict\";\n2\t// External, CSP-'self' script (no inline anything). The token is injected into the page as a data\n3\t// attribute on <body> (unreadable cross-origin); this reads it and uses it for every request.\n4\tconst TOKEN = document.body.dataset.token || \"\";\n5\tconst H = { headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Token\": TOKEN } };\n6\tconst $ = (s) => document.querySelector(s);\n7\tconst esc = (s) => String(s ?? \"\").replace(/[&<>]/g, (c) => ({ \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\" }[c]));\n8\tconst api = (p) => fetch(p, H).then((r) => r.json());\n9\t\n10\tasync function refresh() {\n11\t  const s = await api(\"/api/snapshot\");\n12\t  const k = $(\"#kill\");\n13\t  k.textContent = \"kill: \" + s.kill_switch;\n14\t  k.className = s.kill_switch === \"ENGAGED\" ? \"kill-on\" : \"kill-off\";\n15\t  const q = $(\"#queue\");\n16\t  if (!s.pending_approvals || !s.pending_approvals.length) {\n17\t    q.innerHTML = '<span class=\"empty\">nothing awaiting approval</span>';\n18\t  } else {\n19\t    q.innerHTML = s.pending_approvals.map((a) => `<div class=\"item\">\n20\t      <div class=\"meta\">seq ${a.seq} · ${esc(a.tier)} · ${esc(a.kind)} · ${esc(a.agent || \"\")}</div>\n21\t      <div class=\"subj\">${esc(a.subject || \"(no subject)\")}</div>\n22\t      <button class=\"approve\" data-act=\"approve\" data-seq=\"${a.seq}\">Approve</button>\n23\t      <button class=\"deny\" data-act=\"deny\" data-seq=\"${a.seq}\">Deny</button></div>`).join(\"\");\n24\t  }\n25\t  $(\"#activity\").innerHTML = kv(s.recent_by_agent) || '<span class=\"empty\">quiet</span>';\n26\t  $(\"#budget\").innerHTML = Object.entries(s.budget_today || {})\n27\t    .map(([a, v]) => `<b>${esc(a)}</b><span>${v.actions} actions · ${v.interrupts} interrupts</span>`).join(\"\")\n28\t    || '<span class=\"empty\">none</span>';\n29\t  const l = s.ingest_lag || {};\n30\t  $(\"#lag\").innerHTML = `<b>head</b><span>${l.head_seq}</span><b>since checkpoint</b><span>${l.records_since_checkpoint}</span>`;\n31\t}\n32\tconst kv = (o) => Object.entries(o || {}).map(([a, n]) => `<b>${esc(a)}</b><span>${n}</span>`).join(\"\");\n33\t\n34\tasync function act(action, seq) {\n35\t  const r = await fetch(\"/api/action\", {\n36\t    method: \"POST\",\n37\t    headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Token\": TOKEN, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n38\t    body: JSON.stringify({ action, seq }),\n39\t  });\n40\t  const j = await r.json();\n41\t  if (!r.ok) alert(\"action refused: \" + (j.error || r.status));\n42\t  refresh();\n43\t}\n44\t\n45\tasync function showRecord(seq) {\n46\t  const r = await api(\"/api/record/\" + seq);\n47\t  const badge = r.integrity_ok === false\n48\t    ? '<span class=\"chip broken\">INTEGRITY BROKEN</span>'\n49\t    : '<span class=\"chip ok\">verified</span>';\n50\t  $(\"#detail-body\").innerHTML =\n51\t    `<div class=\"prov\">record <b>seq ${r.seq}</b> · ${esc(r.kind)} · entry_hash <b>${esc((r.entry_hash || \"\").slice(0, 24))}</b> ${badge}</div>\n52\t     <div class=\"prov\">${esc(r.integrity_reason || \"\")}</div><pre>${esc(JSON.stringify(r.payload, null, 2))}</pre>`;\n53\t  $(\"#detail\").style.display = \"block\";\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\tfunction feedRow(ev) {\n57\t  const anc = ev.integrity_ok === false ? '<span class=\"chip broken\">broken</span>'\n58\t    : ev.anchored ? '<span class=\"chip anchored\">anchored</span>' : '<span class=\"chip tail\">tail</span>';\n59\t  const el = document.createElement(\"div\");\n60\t  el.className = \"row\";\n61\t  el.dataset.seq = ev.seq;\n62\t  el.innerHTML = `<span class=\"seq\">#${ev.seq}</span><span class=\"kind\">${esc(ev.kind)}</span><span class=\"actor\">${esc(ev.actor)}</span><span class=\"txt\">${esc((ev.text || \"\").slice(0, 140))}</span>${anc}`;\n63\t  return el;\n64\t}\n65\tfunction startStream() {\n66\t  const es = new EventSource(\"/api/stream?token=\" + encodeURIComponent(TOKEN));\n67\t  const feed = $(\"#feed\");\n68\t  es.onmessage = (e) => {\n69\t    const ev = JSON.parse(e.data);\n70\t    feed.prepend(feedRow(ev));\n71\t    while (feed.children.length > 300) feed.lastChild.remove();\n72\t  };\n73\t}\n74\t\n75\t// event delegation (no inline handlers — CSP-safe)\n76\tdocument.addEventListener(\"click\", (e) => {\n77\t  const btn = e.target.closest(\"button[data-act]\");\n78\t  if (btn) return act(btn.dataset.act, Number(btn.dataset.seq));\n79\t  const row = e.target.closest(\".row[data-seq]\");\n80\t  if (row) return showRecord(Number(row.dataset.seq));\n81\t  if (e.target.closest(\"#detail-close\")) $(\"#detail\").style.display = \"none\";\n82\t});\n83\t\n84\trefresh();\n85\tsetInterval(refresh, 4000);\n86\tstartStream();\n87\t"}]},"uuid":"b3d80be9-11f3-46b9-926f-9a9ad4071302","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:36.546Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f2d6975a-92e1-480a-9e49-afff91ce7f26","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"9b768c28-96bb-4f65-9dd9-b345bac612c1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WRw2rDhzVqG2SU3ivCUjk1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Shared authentication for governance events (Phase 6). Every state-changing governance event\n2\t(promotion grant/revoke, kill engage/release, approval approve/deny) carries an Ed25519 signature by\n3\tthe OWNER key over its canonical authenticated CORE fields, plus the signer's pubkey. Verification is\n4\tfail-closed: no trusted key, no signature, a pubkey that isn't the trusted owner key, or a tampered\n5\tcore → NOT authentic. This is the primitive that makes \"zero unauthorized A2/A3\" provable from the\n6\tlog rather than promised (a forged governance event never verifies, so it is ignored).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import Optional, Sequence\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sign, verify_one\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _canon(core: dict) -> bytes:\n15\t    m = canonical_json(core)\n16\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef signed_payload(core: dict, owner_key) -> dict:\n20\t    \"\"\"core = the authenticated fields (must be JSON-canonicalizable). Returns core + {sig, pubkey}.\n21\t    With no owner key, sig/pubkey are None → the event will never verify (a caller that requires\n22\t    authentication must reject it).\"\"\"\n23\t    sig = sign(owner_key.private_key_b64, _canon(core)) if owner_key else None\n24\t    return {**core, \"sig\": sig, \"pubkey\": owner_key.public_key_b64 if owner_key else None}\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef verify_signed(payload: dict, core_fields: Sequence[str], trusted_pubkey: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"True iff `payload` carries a valid owner signature over its declared core fields. Fail-closed.\"\"\"\n29\t    if not trusted_pubkey:\n30\t        return False\n31\t    sig = payload.get(\"sig\")\n32\t    if not sig or payload.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey:\n33\t        return False\n34\t    core = {k: payload.get(k) for k in core_fields}\n35\t    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey, _canon(core), sig)\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"72ba51e2-b689-4a8c-ae93-67cf97366499","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:37.456Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9b768c28-96bb-4f65-9dd9-b345bac612c1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c74b140d-c885-4e29-8333-b4fd9b98e2f7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R5azCSDVjjNL82mBacokqD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SpineTailer (Phase 7, P0.2) — a live tail over the append-only, poll-only spine with an HONEST,\n2\ttwo-layer integrity model (red-pen-hardened: unkeyed hashes alone cannot resist a writer who can\n3\trecompute them):\n4\t\n5\t  • `integrity_ok` (CHEAP, per-poll, unkeyed) — per-record binding+derivation (`verify_record`) plus\n6\t    cross-record LINKAGE (this record's `prev_hash` == the last emitted `entry_hash`). This catches\n7\t    ACCIDENTAL corruption, a naive stale-field tamper, a deletion/reorder/gap. It does NOT resist a\n8\t    file-writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash (a tip tamper or a forward-cascaded fork stays\n9\t    internally consistent) — so it is necessary but not sufficient, and is never presented as proof\n10\t    of authenticity on its own.\n11\t\n12\t  • `anchored` (KEYED, via `check_anchor`) — the owner-SIGNED head (`checkpoint.classify_head` /\n13\t    `verify_head`, Ed25519 1-of-1 trust root, monotonic `last_seq`) notarizes the prefix\n14\t    [0 .. signed_last_seq]. A recompute-fork or truncation AT OR BELOW the signed head is\n15\t    unforgeable without the owner key and is caught here; a monotonic high-water catches a rollback\n16\t    of even the un-notarized tail. Records with seq > signed_last_seq are the FRESH, not-yet-keyed\n17\t    tail — emitted `anchored=False` (well-formed but not tamper-proof), keyed at the next `sigil sign`.\n18\t\n19\tA consumer (UI/mobile) shows `integrity_ok=False` as \"integrity broken\", and treats only\n20\t`anchored=True` records as owner-notarized truth; the un-notarized tail is labelled as such, never\n21\tdressed up as proven. One primitive, two consumers — the UI SSE feed and the mobile push notifier —\n22\tboth drive `poll()` on a tick and fan events out via `Broadcaster`.\"\"\"\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\tfrom collections import deque\n26\tfrom typing import Optional\n27\t\n28\tfrom ..config import HEAD_PATH\n29\tfrom ..reuse import SignedChainHead\n30\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _GENESIS_PREV\n31\tfrom .checkpoint import classify_head, trust_root\n32\tfrom .store import SpineStore\n33\tfrom .verify import verify_record\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _shape(record, *, integrity_ok: bool, integrity_reason: str, anchored: bool) -> dict:\n37\t    \"\"\"The cited atom shape emitted to consumers. `integrity_ok` = unkeyed well-formedness;\n38\t    `anchored` = covered by the owner-signed head (keyed). `text` is best-effort, never fabricated.\"\"\"\n39\t    return {\n40\t        \"seq\": record.seq, \"kind\": record.kind, \"source\": record.source, \"actor\": record.actor,\n41\t        \"ts\": record.ts, \"entry_hash\": record.entry_hash, \"text\": record.text(),\n42\t        \"integrity_ok\": integrity_ok, \"integrity_reason\": integrity_reason, \"anchored\": anchored,\n43\t    }\n44\t\n45\t\n46\tclass SpineTailer:\n47\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, since_seq: int = -1,\n48\t                 signed_head: Optional[SignedChainHead] = None, tr=None):\n49\t        \"\"\"Emit records with seq > `since_seq`. `signed_head`/`tr` may be injected (tests); otherwise\n50\t        the owner-signed head is loaded from `HEAD_PATH` + the persisted trust root.\"\"\"\n51\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n52\t        self.cursor = since_seq\n53\t        if since_seq < 0:\n54\t            self._last_hash = _GENESIS_PREV\n55\t        else:\n56\t            anchor = self.store.get(since_seq)\n57\t            self._last_hash = anchor.entry_hash if anchor else _GENESIS_PREV\n58\t        self._injected_head = signed_head\n59\t        self._injected_tr = tr\n60\t        self._high_water = -1                           # monotonic anti-rollback water mark\n61\t        self._signed_last_seq = -1\n62\t        self.check_anchor()                             # establish the initial keyed anchor (reads disk)\n63\t\n64\t    def poll(self) -> list[dict]:\n65\t        \"\"\"Newly-appended records (seq > cursor), each with the cheap `integrity_ok` verdict and the\n66\t        `anchored` (keyed) flag from the last `check_anchor`. On an integrity failure the event is\n67\t        emitted flagged (not dropped); the cursor still advances so the feed never re-emits or stalls.\"\"\"\n68\t        out: list[dict] = []\n69\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=self.cursor):\n70\t            atom_ok, reason = verify_record(r)\n71\t            if r.prev_hash != self._last_hash:\n72\t                ok = False\n73\t                reason = f\"linkage break at seq {r.seq}: prev_hash != last emitted entry_hash (rewind/fork/gap)\"\n74\t            else:\n75\t                ok = atom_ok\n76\t            out.append(_shape(r, integrity_ok=ok, integrity_reason=reason,\n77\t                              anchored=(r.seq <= self._signed_last_seq)))\n78\t            self.cursor = r.seq\n79\t            self._last_hash = r.entry_hash\n80\t        return out\n81\t\n82\t    def check_anchor(self) -> dict:\n83\t        \"\"\"The KEYED verdict. Verify the owner-signed head over the current chain (unforgeable without\n84\t        the owner key): a recompute-fork or truncation at/below the signed head → `anchor_ok=False`;\n85\t        a monotonic high-water rollback of even the un-notarized tail → `rollback` set. Updates\n86\t        `signed_last_seq` so `poll()` can flag records beyond it as the un-notarized tail.\"\"\"\n87\t        entries = self.store.entries()                  # FRESH read from disk (not the cached head)\n88\t        head_seq = entries[-1].seq if entries else -1\n89\t        rollback = None\n90\t        if head_seq < self._high_water:\n91\t            rollback = f\"ROLLBACK: on-disk head seq {head_seq} < monotonic high-water {self._high_water}\"\n92\t        else:\n93\t            self._high_water = head_seq\n94\t        head, tr = self._resolve_head()\n95\t        if head is None:\n96\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n97\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback,\n98\t                    \"reason\": \"no signed checkpoint — the live tail is well-formed but UN-NOTARIZED \"\n99\t                              \"(run `sigil sign` to anchor)\"}\n100\t        try:\n101\t            ok, reason = classify_head(head, entries, tr)\n102\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a malformed head must FAIL closed, never crash the feed\n103\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n104\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback,\n105\t                    \"reason\": f\"anchor unverifiable ({e})\"}\n106\t        self._signed_last_seq = head.last_seq if ok else -1\n107\t        return {\"anchor_ok\": ok and rollback is None, \"signed_last_seq\": self._signed_last_seq,\n108\t                \"rollback\": rollback, \"reason\": reason}\n109\t\n110\t    def _resolve_head(self):\n111\t        if self._injected_head is not None:\n112\t            return self._injected_head, self._injected_tr\n113\t        try:\n114\t            if not HEAD_PATH.exists():\n115\t                return None, None\n116\t            head = SignedChainHead.model_validate_json(HEAD_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n117\t            return head, trust_root()\n118\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no/broken head → unkeyed live tail (honest, fail-closed)\n119\t            return None, None\n120\t\n121\t\n122\tclass Broadcaster:\n123\t    \"\"\"Fan-out of tail events to N subscribers with bounded per-subscriber queues. On overflow the\n124\t    oldest events are dropped and the subscriber is marked `lagged` so it resyncs from the cursor\n125\t    (never a fabricated 'you saw everything').\"\"\"\n126\t    def __init__(self, maxlen: int = 512):\n127\t        self.maxlen = maxlen\n128\t        self._subs: dict[int, deque] = {}\n129\t        self._lagged: dict[int, bool] = {}\n130\t        self._next_id = 0\n131\t\n132\t    def subscribe(self) -> int:\n133\t        sid = self._next_id\n134\t        self._next_id += 1\n135\t        self._subs[sid] = deque(maxlen=self.maxlen)\n136\t        self._lagged[sid] = False\n137\t        return sid\n138\t\n139\t    def unsubscribe(self, sid: int) -> None:\n140\t        self._subs.pop(sid, None)\n141\t        self._lagged.pop(sid, None)\n142\t\n143\t    def publish(self, events: list[dict]) -> None:\n144\t        for sid, q in self._subs.items():\n145\t            for ev in events:\n146\t                if len(q) == self.maxlen:        # would drop the oldest → the subscriber lagged\n147\t                    self._lagged[sid] = True\n148\t                q.append(ev)\n149\t\n150\t    def drain(self, sid: int) -> tuple[list[dict], bool]:\n151\t        \"\"\"Return (events, lagged) and clear both. `lagged=True` tells the consumer to resync.\"\"\"\n152\t        q = self._subs.get(sid)\n153\t        if q is None:\n154\t            return [], False\n155\t        events = list(q)\n156\t        q.clear()\n157\t        lagged = self._lagged.get(sid, False)\n158\t        self._lagged[sid] = False\n159\t        return events, lagged\n160\t"}]},"uuid":"930897cd-df2a-40b1-b862-802b0a169670","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:46.040Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c74b140d-c885-4e29-8333-b4fd9b98e2f7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"4dce6b3c-77cb-4243-9e56-b12291f8ee2d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Su58pGpaUQ1v4hdg57xaTL","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL runtime configuration. All paths local-first, owner-only.\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\timport os\n5\tfrom pathlib import Path\n6\t\n7\t# --- data root (runtime, 0700) --------------------------------------------------------\n8\tSIGIL_HOME = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_HOME\", str(Path.home() / \".sigil\")))\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _load_env_file() -> None:\n12\t    \"\"\"Load persisted `KEY=VALUE` settings from ~/.sigil/sigil.env so a value set once\n13\t    (e.g. SIGIL_QDRANT_URL for server mode) reaches BOTH the CLI and the MCP server that\n14\t    Claude spawns without an explicit env. The real environment always wins (setdefault).\"\"\"\n15\t    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n16\t    if not f.exists():\n17\t        return\n18\t    for line in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n19\t        line = line.strip()\n20\t        if not line or line.startswith(\"#\") or \"=\" not in line:\n21\t            continue\n22\t        k, _, v = line.partition(\"=\")\n23\t        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n24\t\n25\t\n26\t_load_env_file()\n27\t\n28\tSPINE_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"spine.jsonl\"\n29\tHEAD_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"head.json\"\n30\tKEYS_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"keys\"\n31\tCACHE_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"cache\"\n32\t\n33\t# --- ingestion sources ----------------------------------------------------------------\n34\tCLAUDE_PROJECTS = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_CLAUDE_PROJECTS\", str(Path.home() / \".claude\" / \"projects\")))\n35\t# Phase-0a thin slice: PENTEST-main only. Slugified cwd = dir name under ~/.claude/projects.\n36\tPROJECT_ALLOWLIST = os.environ.get(\n37\t    \"SIGIL_PROJECT_ALLOWLIST\", \"-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main\"\n38\t).split(\",\")\n39\t# ephemeral CRUCIBLE-engine runs to filter out\n40\tEPHEMERAL_PREFIXES = (\"-tmp-crucible-cc-\", \"-tmp-\")\n41\t\n42\t# --- vectors (Qdrant, local/embedded — no server, no Docker; in-process, file-backed) -\n43\tQDRANT_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"qdrant\"                       # embedded local-mode store\n44\tQDRANT_URL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_URL\", \"\")       # set to switch to a server later\n45\tQDRANT_COLLECTION = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_COLLECTION\", \"sigil_memory\")\n46\tEMBED_MODEL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_EMBED_MODEL\", \"BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5\")\n47\tEMBED_DIM = 384\n48\t\n49\t# --- graph (Kùzu, deterministic mirror of the spine) ----------------------------------\n50\tGRAPH_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"graph\"                          # base; holds current/ + staging/ (atomic swap)\n51\t\n52\t# --- identity / scope -----------------------------------------------------------------\n53\tSCOPE = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_SCOPE\", \"sigil\")          # the owner's single scope\n54\tOWNER_KEY_ID = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_OWNER_KEY_ID\", \"owner\")\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef ensure_dirs() -> None:\n58\t    for d in (SIGIL_HOME, SPINE_PATH.parent, KEYS_DIR, CACHE_DIR):\n59\t        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n60\t        try:\n61\t            os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n62\t        except OSError:\n63\t            pass\n64\t"}]},"uuid":"070d9f9d-d2ce-4ed2-b3ad-17bc52fcdff2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:46.187Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4dce6b3c-77cb-4243-9e56-b12291f8ee2d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d2f3e8a3-b88f-4f83-8ce6-540ce23ae7a2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KTbdE28HmQURrZy5ESN9Ct","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom typing import Optional\n12\t\n13\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n14\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n15\t\n16\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n17\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass KillSwitch:\n21\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n22\t        self.store = store\n23\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n24\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n25\t\n26\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n27\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n28\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n29\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n30\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n31\t\n32\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n33\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n34\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n35\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n39\t        engaged = False\n40\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n41\t            p = r.payload\n42\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n43\t                continue\n44\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n45\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n46\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n47\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n48\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n49\t        return engaged\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"5511f456-937c-4aaa-b2a0-0368ffc41d87","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:08:58.033Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d2f3e8a3-b88f-4f83-8ce6-540ce23ae7a2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"9c2eb80b-94c4-4a15-824a-00fa92fb2b58","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"9d13eed4-cf8d-4421-9c14-b807eb2edcdb","agentId":"a54575d3079b528d8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EM713n29cAcx6Yx69oPweR","type":"tool_result","content":"2:ask`), so a voice request crosses the SAME T0 router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as any\n4:KERNEL's answer body (the bracketed [T0…]/[WARDEN…] status lines are stripped).\"\"\"\n28:class KernelDispatch:\n29:    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 60):\n37:    def send(self, text: str) -> str:\n---STORE append/next_seq---\n    def append(\n        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n        ts: str | None = None,\n    ) -> int:\n        content = {\n            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n        }\n        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n        record = {\n            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n        }\n        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n        self._last = entry\n        return entry.seq\n\n    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n        if not self.path.exists():\n            return\n        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line:\n                    continue\n                d = json.loads(line)\n                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n\n    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n            if r.seq == seq:\n                return r\n            if r.seq > seq:\n                break\n        return None\n\n    @property\n    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n\n    def count(self) -> int:\n        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9a811702-9020-4505-8273-559702a6c897","timestamp":"2026-07-18T23:09:02.061Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9c2eb80b-94c4-4a15-824a-00fa92fb2b58","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

I explored every named primitive and the two template modules. Here is the design.\n\nNote: I did not modify anything (read-only planning). The plan below is my deliverable — I am not writing it to a report file.\n\n---\n\n# SIGIL Phone Companion — Core Implementation Plan (transport + PWA + pairing + CLI)\n\n## Orientation: what already exists (verified) and what each slice must NOT re-invent\n\nThe engine is done. The bridge already has a **tested library core with no socket**. Every slice below is a thin, reviewable wrapper over existing owner-signed cores. The single most important reuse fact: **`sigil/ui/server.py` is a working two-plane stdlib HTTP server** and **`sigil/bridge/daemon.py::BridgeDaemon`** already exposes exactly the four operations the phone needs. Slice 1 is `ui/server.py` re-pointed at a `bind_ok` address with device-signature auth substituted for the printed token; nothing structural is new.\n\nReuse inventory (exact symbols):\n- `sigil/bridge/daemon.py`: `BridgeDaemon` (`.pending()`, `.submit_device_approval(payload)`, `.panic_engage(by=)`, `.relay(text)`), `bind_ok(addr)`.\n- `sigil/bridge/notifier.py`: `PushNotifier(store, since_seq=).poll()`.\n- `sigil/ui/server.py`: `Handler`, `UIServer`, `_token_ok`, `_action_ok`, `_send`/`_json`/`_deny`, `_sse`, `_serve_static`, `_serve_index`, `_record`, the CSP/nosniff/no-referrer header set, `_MAX_BODY=65536`, `timeout=30`, `do_GET`/`do_POST` routing.\n- `sigil/mesh/registry.py`: `authorize_device`, `revoke_device`, `authorized_devices`, `DeviceApprover`, `_DEV_CORE`, `DEV_SIGNAL`.\n- `sigil/agents/approvals.py`: `verify_approval`, `pending`, `_approval_message`, `SIGNAL=\"governor.approval\"`.\n- `sigil/reuse`: `canonical_json`, `sha256_hex`, `sign`, `verify_one`, `generate_keypair`, `KeyPair`.\n- `sigil/governor/identity.py`: `ensure_owner_keypair`, `owner_pubkey`, `owner_keypair`.\n- `sigil/governor/authn.py`: `signed_payload`, `verify_signed` (the canonical-core + pubkey + sig pattern the envelope mirrors).\n- `sigil/spine/tail.py`: `SpineTailer(store, since_seq=).poll()` (emits `{seq,kind,source,actor,ts,entry_hash,text,integrity_ok,integrity_reason,anchored}`).\n- `sigil/dashboard.py`: `snapshot(store)`. `sigil/spine/verify.py`: `verify_record`. `sigil/config.py`: `SPINE_PATH`, `SIGIL_HOME`, `ensure_dirs`.\n- `sigil/cli.py`: arg patterns (`cmd_serve`, `cmd_approve`, `cmd_host`, `cmd_warden`) and the `sub.add_parser(...).set_defaults(fn=...)` scaffold.\n\nFiles to create: `sigil/bridge/server.py`, `sigil/bridge/envelope.py`, `sigil/bridge/webapp/{manifest.json, service-worker.js, index.html, app.js, style.css}`, `sigil/bridge/webapp/vendor/{noble-ed25519.min.js, qr.min.js}`, `tests/test_bridge.py`.\nFiles to modify: `sigil/cli.py` (new subcommands), `sigil/bridge/__init__.py` (exports).\n\n---\n\n## Slice 2 (build first): `sigil/bridge/envelope.py` — the device-signed request envelope\n\nThis is the auth keystone for panic/relay/reads, so it is designed and built **before** the server even though the prompt numbers it 2. It removes any need for a wire bearer secret.\n\n**Shape.** A canonical, replay-resistant request:\n```\ncore = {\"v\":1, \"device\": <device_pubkey_b64>, \"action\": <str>, \"args\": <dict>, \"nonce\": <int>, \"ts\": <iso8601>}\nenvelope = {**core, \"sig\": sign(device_priv, canonical_json(core))}\n```\n`action` ∈ {`\"panic\"`, `\"relay\"`, `\"read:snapshot\"`, `\"read:pending\"`, `\"read:record\"`, `\"read:stream\"`}. Reuse `canonical_json` + `verify_one` exactly the way `_approval_message`/`verify_approval` do — do **not** invent a new signing scheme.\n\n**Functions (all pure, store-injectable for tests):**\n- `envelope_message(core) -> bytes` = `canonical_json(core)` (byte-identical to what the browser signs — this is the JS/Python parity contract).\n- `verify_envelope(payload, authorized:set) -> (ok, core|reason)`: `payload[\"device\"] in authorized` **and** `verify_one(device, envelope_message(core), sig)`. Fail-closed on missing/foreign key (mirror `verify_approval` lines 47–50).\n- `device_nonce_highwater(store, device_pubkey) -> int`: scan the spine for prior accepted **envelope-receipt** records for this device, return the max nonce (default 0). This reuses the monotonic-high-water pattern already shipped in `cli.py::cmd_warden_anchor_get`.\n- `consume(store, payload, authorized, *, strict_nonce:bool) -> core`: verify signature, and for effectful actions require `core[\"nonce\"] > device_nonce_highwater(...)`; raise on replay. Returns the verified core so the server can act, then the server records a receipt.\n\n**Nonce policy (resolves the \"panic/relay are not target_seq-bound\" gap):**\n- `relay`: **strict monotonic nonce required** — it crosses the WARDEN gate via `KernelDispatch` and can produce a real effect; a replay must never re-run a command.\n- `panic`: nonce checked but **idempotent** — re-halting is harmless (fail-safe direction, matching `killswitch.py`'s \"any engage halts\"). Recommend still requiring a valid envelope so a random tunnel peer can't trivially DoS-halt, but accept that a replayed panic just re-halts.\n- reads: valid signature + **timestamp window** (e.g. ±120 s) rather than a strict counter, so concurrent reads don't fight over the counter and read-replay (re-fetching a snapshot) is bounded, not catastrophic.\n\n**Receipts (where the high-water lives):** the server appends a small `mesh.request` receipt record `{signal:\"mesh.request\", device, action, nonce, ts, tier:\"A0\", decision:\"auto\"}` on every accepted effectful envelope. `device_nonce_highwater` derives from these. This keeps `daemon.py` unchanged (do not destabilize the tested core) — the server orchestrates verify → dispatch → receipt.\n\n**Invariants:** no shared secret anywhere; auth pinned to owner-minted device keys (same trust model as approvals, RP-APPROVAL-2); replay-resistant for effectful actions; fail-closed on unknown/revoked device (because `authorized` is recomputed per request from `authorized_devices`).\n\n**Adversarial lenses:** (a) replay a captured relay envelope → refused by nonce high-water. (b) revoke a device mid-session → next envelope refused because `authorized_devices` drops it. (c) canonicalization mismatch between JS and Python → signature fails closed (never opens). (d) nonce overflow / negative nonce / non-int → reject. (e) a valid read envelope reused as a relay envelope → `action` is inside the signed core, so swapping it invalidates the signature.\n\n**Verify:** unit tests with `generate_keypair()` + `tempfile` SpineStore: good envelope verifies; foreign key refused; replayed nonce refused; revoked device refused; tampered `action`/`args` refused.\n\n---\n\n## Slice 1: `sigil/bridge/server.py` — the WireGuard-bound transport\n\nFork the structure of `ui/server.py` (`ThreadingHTTPServer` + `BaseHTTPRequestHandler`), keep every hardening header verbatim, and change only three things: the **bind address**, the **auth mechanism** (device envelope, not printed token), and the **allowlist derivation** (from the actual bound address).\n\n**Server class `BridgeServer(ThreadingHTTPServer)`** (mirror `UIServer.__init__` lines 40–49):\n- Constructor asserts `bind_ok(addr)` and refuses otherwise (reuse `bind_ok` — the daemon docstring names it as the mandatory predicate). This is the `0.0.0.0`/public guard.\n- `port = self.server_address[1]`; build `self.allowed_hosts = frozenset({f\"{addr}:{port}\"})` and `self.allowed_origins = frozenset({f\"http://{addr}:{port}\"})` — **derived from the real WG IP**, not hardcoded loopback. Add the loopback pair too only when `addr` is loopback (dev). This is the anti-rebind adaptation: the allowlist is an **IP-literal:port**, so DNS rebinding to any hostname fails the exact-match `Host`/`Origin` check exactly as `_action_ok` (lines 75–86) already does. Keep the exact-match (no `startswith`) — the `.evil.com` prefix attack (BLOCK-2) is already defended by the reused code.\n- Hold `self.trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()` and `self.spine_path`; `store()` returns a fresh `SpineStore(spine_path)` per request (reuse pattern, line 48–49). Recompute `authorized_devices(store(), trusted_pubkey)` **per request** (never cache — revocation must take effect immediately, like `is_engaged` reads fresh).\n\n**Auth (the CRITICAL DESIGN QUESTION, resolved):** replace `_token_ok` with `_envelope_ok(action, args)`:\n- Read the envelope from header `X-SIGIL-Envelope` (base64 canonical JSON) for GET/POST, or `?env=` for SSE (SSE can't set headers — mirror the `?token=` accommodation at line 72).\n- Verify via `slice-2 verify_envelope` against per-request `authorized_devices`; enforce nonce/timestamp per action class.\n- **Still apply the Origin/Host action gate** (`_action_ok`'s Host-allowlist + exact Origin + Referer-under-origin, lines 75–86) on the POST action plane, as defense-in-depth for a PWA-driven request. Requires a valid read envelope.\n- `GET /api/stream` (SSE) → reuse `_sse` verbatim but feed `PushNotifier`/`SpineTailer` and gate on `?env=`. This is the sharpest divergence from `ui/actions.py`, which signs server-side: here the browser holds the device key and signs; the server only verifies. Approvals need no envelope nonce — `target_seq` binding + `supersedes_id` already defeat replay (RP-APPROVAL-4), and a duplicate approval record is idempotent.\n- `POST /api/panic` → verify panic envelope, call `BridgeDaemon.panic_engage(by=\"phone\")`, append receipt.
